# Agents · R · part 11

1,087 entries, review-security to reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **review-security**: Security reviewer for code review. Detects vulnerabilities using OWASP Top 10 and common vulnerability patterns. Requires diff output, diff stat output, and PR/MR description (if any) as context. ([ttakanawa/dotfiles](https://github.com/ttakanawa/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-security.md))
- **review-security**: Security review of a change with runtime probes in a temp dir — authN/authZ, injection, secrets, dependencies, web hardening, error paths, prototype pollution — and records a review JSON. ([yolol312/goodboy](https://github.com/yolol312/goodboy/blob/HEAD/agents/review-security.md))
- **review-security**: 보안 관점 코드 리뷰 전문 에이전트. 팀 리뷰 시 보안 분석 담당. "보안 리뷰", "security review" 요청 시 사용. ([younwony/TIL](https://github.com/younwony/TIL/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-security.md))
- **review-security-expert**: Review persona: security lens. Use for input-handling, ReDoS, prototype-pollution, supply-chain, publish-hygiene, GitHub Actions workflow exploitation, and trademark/license concerns. ([simiancraft/simiancraft-skills](https://github.com/simiancraft/simiancraft-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/review-security-expert.md) · ★8)
- **review-security-lens**: Independent reviewer persona for SPADES panel reviews. Focuses on auth, injection, secrets, supply chain, and IAM concerns in the Scope and Plan. Spawned by /spades:review; never invoke directly. ([ChrisFmlyc/spades](https://github.com/ChrisFmlyc/spades/blob/HEAD/plugins/spades/agents/review-security-lens.md))
- **review-senior-designer**: Senior product-designer review of Sorrel for pixel-perfection against the design handoff — spacing/type/radius/colour token fidelity, the wizard shell + 7 funnel steps + delivery calendar, mobile-first responsive behaviour, visual hierarchy, and brand consistency through the App* token layer. Read-only; cites file:line + severity. Trigger: "Use review-senior-designer to audit [scope]". Part of the principal-review team — challenge the others; defer runtime/contract issues to them. ([akin-oz/sorrel](https://github.com/akin-oz/sorrel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-senior-designer.md))
- **review-server-owner**: Server-owner review lens for McRPG PRs — YAML config readability, sane defaults, reload-safety, permission-node design, migration/config-version needs. Reviews as a server admin editing config. Returns structured findings to the review orchestrator; never posts comments. ([DiamondDagger590/McRPG](https://github.com/DiamondDagger590/McRPG/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-server-owner.md) · ★37)
- **review-shard**: 技术方案分片评审(三档法)。对指定章节范围独立评审,输出分级问题清单。当需要并行分片评审设计文档时使用。 ([EchoRealm-io/claude-backend-assets](https://github.com/EchoRealm-io/claude-backend-assets/blob/HEAD/plugins/design-standard/agents/review-shard.md))
- **review-shard**: Use when reviewing one bounded list of source files as part of a whole-repository sweep: read every file on the list, report defects with a severity and a location, and write nothing outside your own shard report. ([zak-keown/moe](https://github.com/zak-keown/moe/blob/HEAD/packages/core/agents/review-shard.md) · 2 copies)
- **review-sidecar**: Reviews diff as an external reviewer with no memory of how the code was written. Catches tautological tests, mock-only assertions, missing edge cases, React/Next anti-patterns. Reports; does not fix. ([artyhoo/getff](https://github.com/artyhoo/getff/blob/HEAD/agents/review-sidecar.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **review-signatures**: Reviews the shape of the data the code passes around — method signatures (three or more parameters, out/ref, Try-style bool returns, objects read only partially) read together with every caller, and fields added to shared data types read together with their assignment and read sites ([neolao/claude-plugin-vibe](https://github.com/neolao/claude-plugin-vibe/blob/HEAD/agents/review-signatures.md))
- **review-silent-failures**: You are an elite error handling auditor with zero tolerance for silent failures and inadequate error handling. Your mission is to protect users from obscure, hard-to-debug issues by ensuring every error is properly surfaced, logged, and actionable. ([Kasempiternal/Claude-Agent-System](https://github.com/Kasempiternal/Claude-Agent-System/blob/HEAD/claude-agent-system-plugin/agents/review-silent-failures.md) · ★15)
- **review-silent-failures**: Pre-commit review track: swallowed errors, empty or over-broad catch blocks, inappropriate fallbacks. Read-only — holds no tool that can modify the working tree. ([vbp1/skills](https://github.com/vbp1/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-panel/agents/review-silent-failures.md))
- **review-simplicity**: 代码简洁性审查者——两面查：(A) 新增代码反过度设计（投机性实现、单实现 interface、改动膨胀、冗余防御、架构早熟），(B) 改动文件附近反历史包袱（死代码、过渡式实现、弱引用、无负责人 TODO、v1/v2 双轨、已弃用残留）；每条标「本 PR 引入」或「顺手该清」。派工：被 /review 作为 finder（🧹）并发调用。禁止：修改文件、操作 git、给「暂时保留/加注释标记」类过渡建议。 ([0xBB2B/bb-spec](https://github.com/0xBB2B/bb-spec/blob/HEAD/opencode/agents/review-simplicity.md) · ★5)
- **review-simplicity**: Reviews expression- and function-level convolution — redundant conditions, pointless indirection, non-idiomatic reimplementations, unused generality, and complexity hotspots (cyclomatic complexity, function length, nesting depth) ([neolao/claude-plugin-vibe](https://github.com/neolao/claude-plugin-vibe/blob/HEAD/agents/review-simplicity.md))
- **review-simplification**: Read-only code reviewer - detects over-engineering the diff introduces: needless layers, premature generalization, indirection, future-proofing, dead fallbacks. - **review-simplify**: Pre-commit review track (gate 8): PROPOSES simplifications that preserve behaviour.
